│             │ │ '/home/ubuntu/OmniPerf-Bench/perf-agents-benc… │ /home/ubuntu/
│             │ │ 'file_text': "# Performance Optimization       │
│             │ │ Summary\n\n## Overview\nThis document          │
│             │ │ summarizes the performance optimizations made  │
│             │ │ to the vLLM codebase to improve the runtime of │
│             │ │ distributed operations by optimizing CUDA      │
│             │ │ stream usage.\n\n## Problem Statement\nThe     │
│             │ │ issue was that `torch.cuda.current_stream()`   │
│             │ │ was being called unnecessarily in hot paths,   │
│             │ │ particularly in the `parallel_state.py` file   │
│             │ │ when calling pynccl operations. This added     │
│             │ │ overhead to every all-reduce operation.\n\n##  │
│             │ │ Root Cause\nIn the original code,              │
│             │ │ `parallel_state.py` was explicitly passing     │
│             │ │ `stream=torch.cuda.current_stream()` to pynccl │
│             │ │ operations:\n\n```python\nout =                │
│             │ │ pynccl_comm.all_reduce(input_,                 │
│             │ │ stream=torch.cuda.current_stream())\n```\n\nT… │
│             │ │ caused `torch.cuda.current_stream()` to be     │
│             │ │ called unconditionally, even when:\n1. The     │
│             │ │ communicator was disabled\n2. Custom allreduce │
│             │ │ was used instead\n3. The operation would fall  │
│             │ │ back to PyTorch's all-reduce\n\n##             │
│             │ │ Solution\nThe optimization removes the         │
│             │ │ redundant `stream=torch.cuda.current_stream()` │
│             │ │ parameter and lets the pynccl methods handle   │
│             │ │ getting the current stream when                │
│             │ │ needed:\n\n```python\nout =                    │
│             │ │ pynccl_comm.all_reduce(input_)\n```\n\nThe     │
│             │ │ pynccl methods already have the logic to get   │
│             │ │ the current stream when `stream is             │
│             │ │ None`:\n\n```python\nif stream is None:\n      │
│             │ │ stream =                                       │
│             │ │ torch.cuda.current_stream()\n```\n\nThis       │
│             │ │ ensures that `torch.cuda.current_stream()` is  │
│             │ │ only called when actually needed.\n\n##        │
│             │ │ Changes Made\n\n### 1.                         │
│             │ │ vllm/distributed/parallel_state.py\n- **Line   │
│             │ │ 360-363**: Removed explicit                    │
│             │ │ `stream=torch.cuda.current_stream()`           │
│             │ │ parameter\n- **Impact**: Reduces overhead by   │
│             │ │ avoiding unnecessary `current_stream()`        │
│             │ │ calls\n- **Benefit**: The call is now only     │
│             │ │ made inside the pynccl method when the         │
│             │ │ communicator is active\n\n### 2.               │
│             │ │ vllm/distributed/device_communicators/pynccl.… │
│             │ │ **Line 121-123**: Added clarifying comment     │
│             │ │ about stream usage\n- **Impact**: No           │
│             │ │ functional change, just documentation          │
│             │ │ improvement\n- **Benefit**: Makes it clear     │
│             │ │ that the method uses the current stream by     │
│             │ │ default\n\n### 3. vllm/utils.py\n- **Lines     │
│             │ │ 1947-2000**: Added helper functions for stream │
│             │ │ management:\n  - `get_current_stream()`:       │
│             │ │ Wrapper for `torch.cuda.current_stream()`\n  - │
│             │ │ `get_stream_device()`: Get device associated   │
│             │ │ with a stream\n  - `wait_stream()`: Wait for a │
│             │ │ stream to complete\n  - `record_event()`:      │
│             │ │ Record a CUDA event on a stream\n- **Impact**: │
│             │ │ Provides reusable utilities for stream         │
│             │ │ operations\n- **Benefit**: Improves code       │
│             │ │ consistency and maintainability\n\n##          │
│             │ │ Performance Impact\n\n### Expected             │
│             │ │ Improvements\n1. **Reduced overhead**:         │
│             │ │ Eliminates redundant                           │
│             │ │ `torch.cuda.current_stream()` calls in the hot │
│             │ │ path\n2. **Better performance**: Especially    │
│             │ │ noticeable in distributed training with        │
│             │ │ frequent all-reduce operations\n3. **Improved  │
│             │ │ code clarity**: The intent is clearer - use    │
│             │ │ the current stream by default\n\n###           │
│             │ │ Measurement\nThe optimization reduces the      │
│             │ │ number of `torch.cuda.current_stream()` calls  │
│             │ │ by:\n- One call per all-reduce operation (when │
│             │ │ custom allreduce is not used)\n- One call per  │
│             │ │ all-reduce operation (when pynccl is disabled, │
│             │ │ as the call is now deferred)\n\n##             │

│             │ │ operations by avoiding unnecessary             │
